# Brackenshire template images (saved as text so they survive between chats)

These are the SpriteCook template images Sam approved on 4 Oct 2026, stored as base64 PNG (colours reduced to 128, otherwise unchanged). To get a file back: copy the base64 for an image into `x.b64` and run `base64 -d x.b64 > upNN.png`, or use the snippet at the end. Numbering matches `art/tpl/upNN.png` in `claude/brackenshire-art-tools.md`. The tall lab and gym raw image (up18) is too large to store here; the finished lab and gym sprites made from it are inside the game build (`ASSET_SRC.h_lab_hd`, `h_gym_hd`).

## Restore all images

```python
import re, base64
doc = open("brackenshire-template-images.md").read()
for n, b64 in re.findall(r"## up(\d\d):.*?```\n(.*?)\n```", doc, re.S):
    open(f"up{n}.png", "wb").write(base64.b64decode(b64.replace("\n", "")))
```
